- **understand-intent**: Use before planning or implementation when a request is ambiguous, underspecified, or has multiple plausible outcomes. Turn it into a small, user-confirmed intent. ([ChristianPavilonis/dotfiles](https://github.com/ChristianPavilonis/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/pi/.pi/agent/skills/understand-intent) · ★1)
- **understand-intent**: Make sure you understand what the user really means before planning, specifying or building anything - especially the depth behind their words. Use at the start of every planning phase (idea review, research, requirements/PRD, architecture), whenever a request names examples ("tools like X and Y", "for example", "etc."), describes one scenario that stands for a broader need, or when the user corrects how you understood them. Produces an Understanding check the user confirms, so examples are not mistaken for complete lists and single cases are not mistaken for the whole requirement. ([Vinay7766/omni-orchestrator](https://github.com/Vinay7766/omni-orchestrator/tree/HEAD/skills/understand-intent) · ★1)
- **understand-intent**: Decodes the user's true underlying intent, unstated goals, and core vision across ANY domain or medium—including teaching and lesson planning, office forms and administrative tasks, creative writing, visual diagrams/sketches, and software engineering. Use whenever a user provides materials (documents, images, worksheets, forms, notes, or code) with brief, informal, or incomplete requests. Teaches the agent to conduct context archaeology, infer the target audience and outcome, think outside the box, and advance the user's vision without forcing inappropriate technical or generic AI boilerplate. ([bckflpboys/voxcode-skills](https://github.com/bckflpboys/voxcode-skills/tree/HEAD/understand-intent))
- **understand-intent**: Confirm what a coding change should actually accomplish via a short plain-language exchange (not a spec doc), then record it so Vouch can verify against it. Use at the start of a non-trivial feature/fix, when the user's request is ambiguous, or when they ask to capture intent. ([divyanshkoolwal-sketch/vouch](https://github.com/divyanshkoolwal-sketch/vouch/tree/HEAD/skills/understand-intent))
- **understand-intent**: Detect user's intent from their message and activate the right workflow silently. ([shivamchopra7/claude-skill-directory](https://github.com/shivamchopra7/claude-skill-directory/tree/HEAD/skills/agent/understand-intent))
- **understand-intent-coder**: Decodes developer intent with deep focus on application coding, UI/UX design coherence, and defensive security best practices. Use when building or updating frontend components, backend routes, forms, file handlers, or full-stack features. Instructs the agent to perform code archaeology on existing styling tokens, component hierarchies, and auth patterns, proactively apply OWASP security defaults and UX polish, and build features that integrate seamlessly without breaking the author's vision or architecture. ([bckflpboys/voxcode-skills](https://github.com/bckflpboys/voxcode-skills/tree/HEAD/understand-intent-coder))
- **understand-intent-engineer**: Decodes unstated technical requirements, mathematical invariants, algorithmic goals, and systems architecture from terse developer requests, WIP code, equations, or scientific scripts. Hyper-specialized for software engineering, computer science, mathematics, physics, and high-performance computing. Instructs the agent to perform deep technical archaeology, anticipate race conditions, memory leaks, numerical instability, and algorithmic complexity ($O(N)$), and implement mathematically sound, idiomatic engineering solutions. ([bckflpboys/voxcode-skills](https://github.com/bckflpboys/voxcode-skills/tree/HEAD/understand-intent-engineer))

- **understand-mcps**: WHAT: Claude Code MCPs — external tools with state, typed data, control systems WHEN: Understanding or building MCPs ([sancovp/host-aios](https://github.com/sancovp/host-aios/tree/HEAD/skills/understand-mcps) · 2 copies)
- **understand-mcs_lock**: **CALL NUMBER:** `concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor.mcs_lock` **DEFINITION:** Queued lock where each waiting thread spins on a locally-owned node linked into a queue; cache-friendly with only O(1) bus traffic per lock acquisition; named after Mellor-Crummey and Scott. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-mcs_lock))
- **understand-mcs_lock_enqueue**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_spinlock.mcs_lock_enqueue` **DEFINITION:** Operation appending a thread-local qnode to the queue tail; atomically CASes qnode into tail; on CAS failure, completes the predecessor's link and retries; ensures FIFO acquisition order. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-mcs_lock_enqueue))
- **understand-mcs_lock_qnode**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_spinlock.mcs_lock_qnode` **DEFINITION:** Thread-local queue node containing locked flag and successor pointer; must be cache-aligned and allocated per-thread to enable local spinning without shared contention. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-mcs_lock_qnode))
- **understand-mcs_lock_release**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_spinlock.mcs_lock_release` **DEFINITION:** Operation by which a thread releases the lock; sets own qnode.locked to false to unblock spinning successor; if successor exists in qnode.next, responsibility transfers to successor's own spin; cache-friendly O(1) handoff. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-mcs_lock_release))

([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-memory_order_relaxed))
- **understand-memory_order_relaxed_atomicity_guarantee**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_c11_memory_model.memory_order_relaxed_atomicity_guarantee : deep_synchronizes_with(20), deep_indivisibility_prope(9), deep_happens_before_relat(7), concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor(5)` **DEFINITION:** The indivisibility guarantee that a relaxed atomic operation completes as a single indivisible step; no intermediate state is observable by other threads during ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-memory_order_relaxed_atomicity_guarantee))
- **understand-memory_order_relaxed_coherence_per_location**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_c11_memory_model.memory_order_relaxed_coherence_per_location` **DEFINITION:** The property that each atomic variable maintains its own independent coherence order but this order is not coordinated with coherence orders of other variables; operations on separate variables are totally unordered with respect to each other. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-memory_order_relaxed_coherence_per_location))
- **understand-memory_order_relaxed_vs_acquire**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_c11_memory_model.memory_order_relaxed_vs_acquire : deep_synchronizes_with(20), deep_indivisibility_prope(9), deep_happens_before_relat(7), concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor(5)` **DEFINITION:** The ordering contrast where acquire_semantics prevents subsequent operations from being reordered before the acquire, while memory_order_relaxed permits all such reorder ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-memory_order_relaxed_vs_acquire))
- **understand-memory_order_release**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_synchronizes_with.memory_order_release : deep_happens_before_relat(6)` **DEFINITION:** Memory ordering barrier ensuring all loads and stores before the barrier in program order cannot be reordered after it, making prior writes visible to acquiring threads. - **understand-quorum**: **CALL NUMBER:** `distributed_consensus_and_replication_protocols.quorum : deep_raft(6), deep_consensus(4)` **DEFINITION:** Subset of nodes sufficient for reads or writes; typically majority (n/2+1) for strong consistency. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_distributed_consensus_and_replication_protocols/understand-quorum))
- **understand-raft**: **CALL NUMBER:** `distributed_consensus_and_replication_protocols.raft : deep_raft(6)` **DEFINITION:** Consensus algorithm designed for understandability, using leader election, log replication, and safety viaterm numbers. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_distributed_consensus_and_replication_protocols/understand-raft))
- **understand-read_committed**: **CALL NUMBER:** `database_transaction_isolation_and_concurrency_c.read_committed : deep_database_transaction(26), deep_isolation_level(4)` **DEFINITION:** An isolation_level where each statement sees only data committed before that statement begins; prevents dirty_read but allows non_repeatable_read and phantom_read. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_database_transaction_isolation_and_concurrency_c/understand-read_committed))
- **understand-read_lock_release**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_isolation_level.read_lock_release : database_transaction_isolation_and_concurrency_c(10), deep_database_transaction(6)` **DEFINITION:** lock management characteristic in read_committed where shared read locks are typically released immediately after each row access rather than held to transaction end ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_database_transaction_isolation_and_concurrency_c/understand-read_lock_release))
- **understand-reads_from_pinecone_index**: **CALL NUMBER:** `?.reads_from_pinecone_index` **DEFINITION:** The data access relationship where fetch or query operations retrieve stored records from a Pinecone index by id or vector similarity. - **understand-recovery_log_entry**: **CALL NUMBER:** `?.recovery_log_entry` **DEFINITION:** A durable record written to the transaction log capturing a single row modification, containing the transaction identifier, the affected row identifier, the before_image, and the after_image; the recovery_subsystem uses these entries to undo uncommitted changes and redo committed changes during crash recovery. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_database_transaction_isolation_and_concurrency_c/understand-recovery_log_entry))
- **understand-ref_pointer**: **CALL NUMBER:** `git_internals_and_the_object_model.ref_pointer` **DEFINITION:** Named file under .git/refs/ containing a SHA-1; represents a branch tip, tag, or remote-tracking branch; updated atomically during operations. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_git_internals_and_the_object_model/understand-ref_pointer))

- **understand-refrigeration_metabolism**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_sourdough_starter.refrigeration_metabolism : sourdough_baking(1)` **DEFINITION:** Reduced metabolic rate of wild_yeast and lactobacillus populations occurring at cold temperatures, producing minimal CO2 and acid output. ([sancovp/dark-factory](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_sourdough_baking/understand-refrigeration_metabolism) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **understand-refused_bequest**: **CALL NUMBER:** `refactoring_catalog_and_code_smells.refused_bequest` **DEFINITION:** A subclass using only some inherited methods, suggesting inheritance hierarchy is wrong or composition preferred. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_refactoring_catalog_and_code_smells/understand-refused_bequest))
- **understand-regular_file_mode**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_tree_object.regular_file_mode` **DEFINITION:** Mode 100644; standard blob-backed file with read permission for all and write for owner; the default for non-executable files committed to Git. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_git_internals_and_the_object_model/understand-regular_file_mode))
- **understand-relaxed_atomicity**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_c11_memory_model.relaxed_atomicity` **DEFINITION:** The sole guarantee of memory_order_relaxed: a load or store completes atomically without torn reads or torn writes; no other constraints are imposed. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-relaxed_atomicity))
- **understand-relaxed_atomicity_guarantee**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_c11_memory_model.relaxed_atomicity_guarantee : concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor(2)` **DEFINITION:** The sole guarantee provided by memory_order_relaxed: the atomic variable is read and written atomically without tearing, but no ordering constraints are imposed on other operations relative to the relaxed operation. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-relaxed_atomicity_guarantee))
- **understand-reordering_freedom_privilege**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_c11_memory_model.reordering_freedom_privilege : concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor(2)` **DEFINITION:** A relaxed atomic operation imposes no constraints on the reordering of memory operations by either the compiler or the hardware; the operation may be reordered with any other operation including non-atomic and other atomic operations. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-reordering_freedom_privilege))
- **understand-repitch**: Load the moment the user signals the last message or current state did not land — "wait, what?", "I'm lost", "hold on", "I don't follow", "re-explain", "say that again" — or asks for a re-pitch. Stop and re-explain at project altitude before doing anything else. For rebuilding their grasp of the whole project, use understand-project. ([mnazaal/dotfiles](https://github.com/mnazaal/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/understand-repitch))
- **understand-replica_synchronization**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_consensus.replica_synchronization : distributed_consensus_and_replication_protocols(8)` **DEFINITION:** Propagation of updates to ensure all replicas converge toward identical state. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_distributed_consensus_and_replication_protocols/understand-replica_synchronization))
- **understand-replicated_log**: **CALL NUMBER:** `distributed_consensus_and_replication_protocols.replicated_log : deep_raft(6)` **DEFINITION:** Ordered log of commands maintained across replicas; foundation of state machine replication. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_distributed_consensus_and_replication_protocols/understand-replicated_log))
- **understand-repo**: Use when understanding a repository or subsystem before reading individual files, including its purpose, runtime boundaries, entry points, representative flows, configuration, tests, and reading path. ([tomwangowa/agent-skills](https://github.com/tomwangowa/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/claude/repo-workflow/skills/understand-repo) · ★2 · 3 copies)
- **understand-repo**: Analyze any repository and generate a self-contained, LiquidGlass-styled HTML study guide plus a Mermaid architecture diagram, a compressed markdown summary, and a structured JSON index. Use when the user asks to "analyze this repo", "generate a study guide", "explain this codebase", or wants a visual onboarding doc for a project. ([crosaless/understand-repo](https://github.com/crosaless/understand-repo/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **understand-request_reply**: **CALL NUMBER:** `enterprise_integration_patterns.request_reply` **DEFINITION:** A message exchange pattern pairing an outgoing request message with a correlated response message returned to the original sender. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_enterprise_integration_patterns/understand-request_reply))
- **understand-requirements**: Establish what a task-splitter run is building. In design mode, conduct a full structured requirements interview covering goals, features, constraints, scope boundaries, and definition of done. In split mode, read most of that out of an existing design doc and ask only what the doc cannot answer. Writes a machine-readable requirements-report.md either way. Use this at the start of every task-splitter run. ([k1nak0/marketplace](https://github.com/k1nak0/marketplace/tree/HEAD/codex/plugins/task-splitter/skills/understand-requirements) · 2 copies)
- **understand-resilience**: **CALL NUMBER:** `resilience_and_reliability_engineering_patterns.resilience` **DEFINITION:** system's ability to recover from failures and continue functioning; bouncing back from disruptions rather than never failing ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_resilience_and_reliability_engineering_patterns/understand-resilience))

- **understand-rmw_ind_001**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_indivisibility_prope.rmw_ind_001 : deep_c11_memory_model(3)` **DEFINITION:** rmw_operation: an atomic fetch_add, fetch_sub, fetch_or, fetch_and, fetch_xor, fetch_max, fetch_min, or fetch_compare_exchange on a C11 atomic variable; the operation atomically reads the current value, computes a new value, and writes it back as a single hardware-level instruction sequence. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-rmw_ind_001))
- **understand-rmw_ind_003**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_indivisibility_prope.rmw_ind_003 : deep_c11_memory_model(3)` **DEFINITION:** modify_phase: the purely computational step wherein the value captured in the read phase is transformed by the operation's specific function (addition, subtraction, bitwise OR, bitwise AND, exclusive OR, maximum, minimum, or conditional replacement); this phase produces the value to be written but d ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-rmw_ind_003))
- **understand-rmw_ind_004**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_indivisibility_prope.rmw_ind_004 : deep_c11_memory_model(3)` **DEFINITION:** write_phase: the final step wherein the result of the modify phase is written atomically to the target variable; this write becomes the new current value visible in the modification order for all subsequent operations. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-rmw_ind_004))
- **understand-rmw_ind_006**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_indivisibility_prope.rmw_ind_006 : deep_c11_memory_model(3)` **DEFINITION:** modification_order_commitment: the serialization of the RMW operation into the global modification order of the target atomic variable at the instant the write_phase becomes visible; this ordering point determines the value returned by concurrent operations. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-rmw_ind_006))
- **understand-rollback**: **CALL NUMBER:** `database_transaction_isolation_and_concurrency_c.rollback : deep_isolation_level(1)` **DEFINITION:** The act of undoing a transaction's effects using undo_log entries, returning the database to its pre-transaction state. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_database_transaction_isolation_and_concurrency_c/understand-rollback))
- **understand-row_level_locking**: **CALL NUMBER:** `database_transaction_isolation_and_concurrency_c.row_level_locking : deep_database_transaction(6), deep_isolation_level(1)` **DEFINITION:** Lock management at the individual row granularity via row_lock entries in lock_manager's lock table; enables high concurrency but increases lock_manager overhead. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_database_transaction_isolation_and_concurrency_c/understand-row_level_locking))
- **understand-rr_definition**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_database_transaction.rr_definition` **DEFINITION:** An isolation_level ensuring that all reads within a transaction see a consistent snapshot of the database as of transaction start; prevents non_repeatable_read by acquiring and holding read locks on accessed rows until transaction end. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_database_transaction_isolation_and_concurrency_c/understand-rr_definition))
- **understand-rr_read_lock**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_database_transaction.rr_read_lock` **DEFINITION:** A shared_lock acquired when a row is read under repeatable_read; held until transaction commits or rolls back, preventing any concurrent transaction from acquiring a write_lock on that row. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_database_transaction_isolation_and_concurrency_c/understand-rr_read_lock))
- **understand-rrf_fusion_score**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_dense_retrieval.rrf_fusion_score` **DEFINITION:** The sum of reciprocal rank values for a given document across all contributing rank lists; the document's position in the fused ranking is determined by descending fusion score. - **understand-rrf_rank_position**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_dense_retrieval.rrf_rank_position` **DEFINITION:** The integer position of an item within a rank list, starting at 1 for the highest-scoring result; used as the denominator in reciprocal rank computation. - **understand-seq_cst_atomic_visibility**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_c11_memory_model.seq_cst_atomic_visibility` **DEFINITION:** The guarantee that all threads observe the effects of a sequentially consistent operation at the same logical point in the global total order; reads observe the most recent write in the total order. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-seq_cst_atomic_visibility))
- **understand-seq_cst_atomic_visibility_happens_before_cutoff_visibility**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_c11_memory_model.seq_cst_atomic_visibility_happens_before_cutoff_visibility : concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor(2)` **DEFINITION:** A seq_cst read cannot observe a write that is ordered after it in the total order; the happens-before relationship established by seq_cst operations enforces a cutoff beyond which writes are not visible. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-seq_cst_atomic_visibility_happens_before_cutoff_visibility))
- **understand-seq_cst_default_atomic_ordering**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_c11_memory_model.seq_cst_default_atomic_ordering : deep_synchronizes_with(20), deep_happens_before_relat(7), concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor(3)` **DEFINITION:** The default memory ordering for std::atomic operations in C11/C++11 when no memory_order is explicitly specified; provides the strongest guarantees without requiring explicit fence code. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-seq_cst_default_atomic_ordering))
- **understand-seq_cst_global_total_order**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_c11_memory_model.seq_cst_global_total_order : deep_synchronizes_with(20), deep_happens_before_relat(7), concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor(3)` **DEFINITION:** A single total order of all sequentially consistent operations that is visible and agreed upon by all threads in the system; the interleaving point where all threads observe the same sequence of operation ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-seq_cst_global_total_order))
- **understand-seq_cst_indivisible_atomicity**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_c11_memory_model.seq_cst_indivisible_atomicity` **DEFINITION:** The property that each sequentially consistent operation appears indivisible and instantaneous to all observers; no intermediate states are visible during the operation. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-seq_cst_indivisible_atomicity))
- **understand-seq_cst_synchronization_protocol**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_c11_memory_model.seq_cst_synchronization_protocol : concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor(1)` **DEFINITION:** The protocol by which sequentially consistent operations establish synchronization points between threads; creates a happens-before relationship across thread boundaries. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-seq_cst_synchronization_protocol))
- **understand-sequentially_consistent**: **CALL NUMBER:** `concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor.sequentially_consistent : deep_synchronizes_with(20), deep_c11_memory_model(16), deep_happens_before_relat(7)` **DEFINITION:** The strongest memory ordering requiring a single total order of all sequentially consistent operations visible to all threads; implied by default for std::atomic operations in C++. - **understand-stream_log**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_event_driven_archite.stream_log` **DEFINITION:** An immutable append-only sequence of events retained on disk or equivalent durable storage; unlike pubsub_message which is ephemeral, events in a stream_log persist for the configured retention period and can be reread from any offset by any consumer group. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_software_architecture_patterns_and_styles/understand-stream_log))
- **understand-stream_offset**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_event_driven_archite.stream_offset` **DEFINITION:** A monotonically increasing integer position marker assigned to each event within a partition, uniquely identifying the event's place in the partition's sequence and enabling consumers to resume reading from a specific point. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_software_architecture_patterns_and_styles/understand-stream_offset))
- **understand-stream_partition**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_event_driven_archite.stream_partition` **DEFINITION:** A distinct, ordered slice of an event stream, identified by a partition key or index, enabling parallel production and consumption; each partition maintains its own offset sequence independently of other partitions. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_software_architecture_patterns_and_styles/understand-stream_partition))
- **understand-structural_pattern**: **CALL NUMBER:** `?.structural_pattern` **DEFINITION:** A design pattern category focused on composing classes and objects into larger structures, defining relationships between entities to build flexible and efficient hierarchies. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_software_design_patterns/understand-structural_pattern))
- **understand-subagents**: WHAT: Claude Code subagents — isolated contexts, persistent memory, worktree isolation, GAN scoring WHEN: Understanding or creating subagents ([sancovp/host-aios](https://github.com/sancovp/host-aios/tree/HEAD/skills/understand-subagents) · 2 copies)
- **understand-subtree_mode**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_tree_object.subtree_mode` **DEFINITION:** Mode 040000; entry points to a nested tree object representing a directory; the tree entry name contains a forward slash but the slash is not stored in the entry itself. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_git_internals_and_the_object_model/understand-subtree_mode))
- **understand-swustcyt-langchain-travel-age**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/analysis/understand-swustcyt-langchain-travel-age) · ★745)
- **understand-symlink_mode**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_tree_object.symlink_mode` **DEFINITION:** Mode 120000; entry points to a blob object containing the target path as raw bytes; the blob content is the symlink destination, not the link data itself. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_git_internals_and_the_object_model/understand-symlink_mode))
- **understand-synchronizes_with**: **CALL NUMBER:** `concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor.synchronizes_with : deep_synchronizes_with(20), deep_happens_before_relat(7), deep_c11_memory_model(1)` **DEFINITION:** A relation between atomic operations in memory models where a release fence on one thread synchronizes with an acquire fence on another thread, establishing happens-before order across threads. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-synchronizes_with))
- **understand-synchronizes_with_absence**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_c11_memory_model.synchronizes_with_absence : concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor(2)` **DEFINITION:** A relaxed atomic operation does not synchronize with any other operation; it establishes no happens-before or synchronizes-with relationship with any thread, including the thread that performed the operation. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-synchronizes_with_absence))
- **understand-system**: Understand one or more local codebases as a system. Resolves explicit targets and the active workspace first, then maps architecture, relationships, real flows, contracts, local runtime/debug context, validation seams, and evidence gaps. ([alexandersumer/config](https://github.com/alexandersumer/config/tree/HEAD/skills/understand-system) · ★1)
- **understand-tagged_union_type**: **CALL NUMBER:** `type_systems_for_working_programmers.tagged_union_type : deep_tagged_union_type(23)` **DEFINITION:** Discriminated union with explicit tag field enabling exhaustive pattern matching. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_type_systems_for_working_programmers/understand-tagged_union_type))
- **understand-task**: Use at intake or when requirements change to clarify intent, constraints and observable acceptance before delegation. ([qxbao/bflow](https://github.com/qxbao/bflow/tree/HEAD/skills/core/understand-task) · ★4)
- **understand-task**: Understand a pasted task step by step before implementation. ([djokobozinov/agent-skills](https://github.com/djokobozinov/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/productivity/understand-task))
- **understand-tbox**: **CALL NUMBER:** `knowledge_graphs_and_ontologies.tbox` **DEFINITION:** The terminological box — the schema-level component of a knowledge base defining classes, properties, and their relations. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_knowledge_graphs_and_ontologies/understand-tbox))
- **understand-te_capturing**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_second_brain.te_capturing` **DEFINITION:** The first phase of the CODE framework where information is quickly captured before it fades from working memory; a frictionless gateway that accepts raw input without requiring immediate structuring. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_personal_knowledge_management_and_the_second_bra/understand-te_capturing))
- **understand-te_distilling**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_second_brain.te_distilling` **DEFINITION:** The process of extracting essential insights from source material through progressive summarization; a compression operation that retains meaning while reducing volume. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_personal_knowledge_management_and_the_second_bra/understand-te_distilling))
- **understand-te_expressing**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_second_brain.te_expressing` **DEFINITION:** The final phase of the CODE framework where accumulated knowledge becomes tangible output; the conversion operation that transforms internal knowledge structures into external artifacts. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_personal_knowledge_management_and_the_second_bra/understand-te_expressing))
- **understand-te_organizing**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_second_brain.te_organizing` **DEFINITION:** The structuring operation that transforms captured material into a retrievable system; grouping related content by semantic relationship to enable future discovery and connection. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_personal_knowledge_management_and_the_second_bra/understand-te_organizing))
- **understand-teams**: WHAT: How to understand and configure agent teams WHEN: Understanding or creating agent teams for parallel work, observation stacks, or multi-agent coordination ([sancovp/host-aios](https://github.com/sancovp/host-aios/tree/HEAD/skills/understand-teams))
- **understand-teams**: WHAT: Agent teams — two patterns: Metacog Shell (peer messaging, self-improving) and World of Skillcraft (shared state, persistent agents) WHEN: Understanding or creating agent teams for parallel work, observation stacks, or multi-agent coordination ([sancovp/twi-jobworld](https://github.com/sancovp/twi-jobworld/tree/HEAD/skills/understand-teams))
- **understand-technology**: Use for explaining materials science and engineering concepts, including material properties, structure–property relationships, processing methods, and performance mechanisms. Generate structured explanations of materials and engineering technologies by covering underlying scientific principles, structure–property relationships, processing methods, and performance characteristics, with clear connections between material composition, microstructure, and functional behavior in practical applications. Use for queries such as "What is X?", "How does X work?", or "Explain the science behind X". ([patsnap/skills](https://github.com/patsnap/skills/tree/HEAD/materials/understand-technology) · ★46)
- **understand-technology-zhcn**: 用于解释材料科学与工程概念，涵盖材料性能、结构-性能关系、加工工艺与性能机理。通过阐述基本科学原理、结构-性能关系、加工工艺与性能特征，并在材料组成、微观结构与实际应用中的功能行为之间建立清晰关联，生成对材料与工程技术的结构化说明。 典型查询： - X 是什么？ - X 的工作原理是什么？ - 解释 X 背后的科学原理 ([patsnap/skills](https://github.com/patsnap/skills/tree/HEAD/materials/understand-technology-zhcn) · ★46)

- **understand-the-problem**: Answer the questions about a task for the user and for you. Cover what the problem is, including sub-problems you discover, who it affects and why they would care, and whether it is the right problem or a symptom of another one. When the task is a goal, a Jira issue, or something in the current project, look it up before answering. Use when the user asks what the problem is, who has this problem, why it matters, why now, whether this is the right problem, to understand the problem, or runs /understand-the-problem. ([Zooce/skills](https://github.com/Zooce/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/understand-the-problem))
- **understand-the-project**: Map an unfamiliar software project before changing it. ([minutechreview/build-with-clarity](https://github.com/minutechreview/build-with-clarity/tree/HEAD/skills/understand-the-project))
- **understand-the-system**: Use when entering a codebase, before any design or estimate, or when your mental model has gone stale — read width AND depth and refresh the C4-level picture; a design from a skim is a guess. - **understand-thesis-related-repository**: Use when reverse-engineering an unfamiliar repository (a paper's codebase, a benchmark, a library) that relates to your thesis, and you want a guided, gradual walkthrough instead of everything explained at once. ([KtfwyCJ/Skills-Shelf](https://github.com/KtfwyCJ/Skills-Shelf/tree/HEAD/skills/research/understand-thesis-related-repository))
- **understand-this**: Acts as a wise, effective tutor that makes the user deeply understand the work just done in a session — a feature, bug fix, PR, refactor, or codebase exploration. Teaches incrementally, checks for mastery before moving on, drills into the why behind the problem and solution, and quizzes the user with AskUserQuestion. Use this skill whenever the user wants to learn, understand, internalize, or be walked through something — e.g. "teach me what we just did", "help me understand this PR/change", "make sure I actually get this", "quiz me on this", "walk me through the design decisions", "onboard m… ([emanualjade/agent-tools](https://github.com/emanualjade/agent-tools/tree/HEAD/skills/understand-this))
- **understand-this-code**: Explain how the code you are about to change works, why it has its current shape, and what a change could break, proving safety claims by running code. Use before changing unfamiliar code, and when spec or architect needs to know what the code already answers. ([harrymadison297/harry-skills](https://github.com/harrymadison297/harry-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/plan/understand-this-code))
- **understand-this-codebase**: 对陌生或半熟的代码建立够用的最小心智模型——按当前目标裁剪范围，顺着端到端流程而非文件清单走，结论标注 已观察/推断/未知。上手新仓库、调查某功能或运行时流程、准备改动时使用。要引证答案的问题归 pstack how/why，只找代码位置归 recon，外部信源调研归 research。 ([nekomorph-woo/hapilon](https://github.com/nekomorph-woo/hapilon/tree/HEAD/resources/skills/understand-this-codebase))
- **understand-thundering_herd_contention_surge**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_exponential_backoff.thundering_herd_contention_surge` **DEFINITION:** A transient spike in the rate of lock or resource acquisition attempts triggered by mass wakeup, producing queue buildup and wasted retry cycles. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-thundering_herd_contention_surge))
- **understand-thundering_herd_retry_coordination**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_exponential_backoff.thundering_herd_retry_coordination` **DEFINITION:** The phenomenon where concurrently woken threads retry a failing operation in near-lockstep, reinforcing the contention surge; mitigated by jitter applied to retry delays. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-thundering_herd_retry_coordination))
- **understand-thundering_herd_wait_set**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_exponential_backoff.thundering_herd_wait_set` **DEFINITION:** A collection of threads blocked on a single synchronization point awaiting a condition; the structural prerequisite for thundering-herd because coordinated wakeup of this set is the failure mode. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-thundering_herd_wait_set))
- **understand-thundering_herd_wakeup_event**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_exponential_backoff.thundering_herd_wakeup_event` **DEFINITION:** A single signal or broadcast that causes ALL members of a wait_set to transition from blocked to runnable simultaneously; the atomic cause of the thundering-herd pattern. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_concurrency_synchronization_primitives_and_memor/understand-thundering_herd_wakeup_event))
- **understand-timezone-offsets**: Pull the latest timezone IDs and their current GMT offsets from Noda Time ([zfox23/WhereWeWere](https://github.com/zfox23/WhereWeWere/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/understand-timezone-offsets) · ★1)
- **understand-tl_checkpoint_record**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_database_transaction.tl_checkpoint_record` **DEFINITION:** A special log_record written periodically by a checkpoint_writer; records the volatile database state at a consistent point, bounding the recovery_manager's replay horizon. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_database_transaction_isolation_and_concurrency_c/understand-tl_checkpoint_record))
- **understand-tl_flush_to_disk**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_database_transaction.tl_flush_to_disk` **DEFINITION:** The act of transferring the contents of tl_log_buffer to durable storage; triggered by transaction commit, tl_log_buffer overflow, or a periodic checkpoint; O(1) or O(n) bounded by buffer size. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_database_transaction_isolation_and_concurrency_c/understand-tl_flush_to_disk))
- **understand-tl_log_record**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_database_transaction.tl_log_record` **DEFINITION:** The fundamental unit of a transaction_log: a single append-only entry recording one atomic change or lifecycle event made by a transaction; identified by an lsn. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_database_transaction_isolation_and_concurrency_c/understand-tl_log_record))
- **understand-tl_recovery_manager**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_database_transaction.tl_recovery_manager` **DEFINITION:** The subsystem that, on system restart, reads the transaction_log from the last tl_checkpoint_record forward; replays committed tl_redo_log entries and rolls back uncommitted transactions using tl_undo_log. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_database_transaction_isolation_and_concurrency_c/understand-tl_recovery_manager))
- **understand-tl_undo_log**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_database_transaction.tl_undo_log` **DEFINITION:** A structured collection of tl_update_record entries chained by a transaction; traversed in reverse order during rollback to restore the before_image of each modified entity. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_database_transaction_isolation_and_concurrency_c/understand-tl_undo_log))
- **understand-tl_update_record**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_database_transaction.tl_update_record` **DEFINITION:** A log_record capturing a before_image and after_image of a modified data page or row; used by both redo_recovery (for durability) and undo_recovery (for atomicity rollback). ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_database_transaction_isolation_and_concurrency_c/understand-tl_update_record))
- **understand-tmdb-schema**: Pull the latest API schema from TheMovieDB ([zfox23/WhereWeWere](https://github.com/zfox23/WhereWeWere/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/understand-tmdb-schema) · ★1)
- **understand-to-remember**: Teach a topic so the user understands it and remembers it. Use when the user wants to learn, study, or understand something, or prepare for an exam or interview. Not for quick lookups, such as what a command or flag does. ([rafaeelricco/dotfiles](https://github.com/rafaeelricco/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/skill/understand-to-remember))
- **understand-token_boundary_marker**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_context_window.token_boundary_marker` **DEFINITION:** The positional delimiter marking the transition from input context to output generation; tokens before this marker are provided context, tokens after are to be generated. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_llm_memory_and_context_engineering/understand-token_boundary_marker))
- **understand-token_endpoint**: **CALL NUMBER:** `oauth_2_0_and_openid_connect.token_endpoint : deep_oauth_2_0(1)` **DEFINITION:** OAuth 2.0 endpoint accepting authorization_code or other grants and returning access_token, refresh_token, and optional id_token. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_oauth_2_0_and_openid_connect/understand-token_endpoint))
- **understand-token_type**: **CALL NUMBER:** `?.token_type` **DEFINITION:** OAuth 2.0 parameter indicating how the access_token is to be presented to the resource server, typically Bearer, specifying the authorization scheme to use in requests. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_oauth_2_0_and_openid_connect/understand-token_type))
- **understand-transaction_state**: **CALL NUMBER:** `database_transaction_isolation_and_concurrency_c.transaction_state : deep_isolation_level(1)` **DEFINITION:** The current phase of a transaction lifecycle: active, partially_committed, committed, aborted; drives lock release and recovery behavior. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_database_transaction_isolation_and_concurrency_c/understand-transaction_state))
- **understand-tree_entry_mode**: **CALL NUMBER:** `git_internals_and_the_object_model.tree_entry_mode : deep_tree_object(8)` **DEFINITION:** Octal mode in tree object: 100644 (regular file), 100755 (executable), 040000 (subtree), 120000 (symlink), 160000 (submodule). ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_git_internals_and_the_object_model/understand-tree_entry_mode))
- **understand-tree_object**: **CALL NUMBER:** `git_internals_and_the_object_model.tree_object : deep_commit_object(8), deep_tree_object(8)` **DEFINITION:** Type-2 git object listing directory entries as (mode, name, sha1) tuples; represents a single directory snapshot at a point in time. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_git_internals_and_the_object_model/understand-tree_object))
- **understand-trivial_getter**: **CALL NUMBER:** `deep_dispensables.trivial_getter` **DEFINITION:** A getter that merely returns an internal field with zero transformation or business meaning. ([sancovp/dark-factory-live](https://github.com/sancovp/dark-factory-live/tree/HEAD/kbworld/state/libraries/_staging_refactoring_catalog_and_code_smells/understand-trivial_getter))

- **understand-until-simple**: Use this skill BEFORE writing code for any task you're tempted to call "hard / tough / complex", any "why is X slow / where's the bottleneck", any "should I optimize Y", any concurrency/perf/kernel/scheduler investigation, or any time you catch yourself hand-waving a root cause. It is the pre-implementation understanding GATE — decompose to the atomic (line/kernel/buffer) level, get MEASURED evidence, let measurement correct your hypotheses, until the problem is SIMPLE and quantified. Only then write code. If it still feels hard, you haven't decomposed enough. ([acupof-ai/arle](https://github.com/acupof-ai/arle/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/understand-until-simple) · ★22)

- **Understanding User Intent**: This skill should be used before planning or implementing any non-trivial request.
